async function loadCurrencyRate(){
  const status=document.getElementById('currencyStatus');
  try{
    if(status){status.classList.remove('error');status.textContent='جاري تحديث سعر الصرف…'}
    let rate=0,date='';
    let res=await fetch('https://api.frankfurter.dev/v2/rate/SAR/KRW',{cache:'no-store'});
    if(res.ok){
      const data=await res.json();
      rate=Number(data?.rate);
      date=data?.date||'';
    }
    if(!rate){
      res=await fetch('https://open.er-api.com/v6/latest/SAR',{cache:'no-store'});
      if(!res.ok)throw new Error('rate');
      const data=await res.json();
      rate=Number(data?.rates?.KRW);
      date=data?.time_last_update_utc||'';
    }
    if(!rate)throw new Error('rate');
    sarToKrwRate=rate;
    convertFromSar();
    const shownRate=new Intl.NumberFormat('en-US',{maximumFractionDigits:2}).format(rate);
    setCurrencyStatus(`1 ريال سعودي = ${shownRate} وون كوري${date?' • آخر تحديث: '+date:''}`);
  }catch(err){
    console.error(err);
    setCurrencyStatus('تعذر تحديث سعر الصرف الآن. تحقق من الاتصال وحاول مرة أخرى.',true);
  }
}

  const box=document.createElement('textarea');
  box.innerHTML=String(value||'');
  return box.value;
}

  const output=document.getElementById('translationTarget');

    const res=await fetch(url,{cache:'no-store'});
    if(!res.ok)throw new Error('translation');
    const data=await res.json();
    const translated=decodeTranslationText(data?.responseData?.translatedText||'').trim();
    if(!translated||Number(data?.responseStatus||200)>=400)throw new Error('translation');
    output.value=translated;

async function translateTextNow(){
  const input=document.getElementById('translationSource');
  const output=document.getElementById('translationTarget');
  const status=document.getElementById('translationStatus');
  const textValue=input.value.trim();
  if(!textValue){status.textContent='اكتب العبارة أولًا.';input.focus();return}
  const c=translationCodes();
  try{
    status.classList.remove('error');
    status.textContent='جاري الترجمة…';
    output.value='';
    const url=`https://api.mymemory.translated.net/get?q=${encodeURIComponent(textValue)}&langpair=${encodeURIComponent(c.source+'|'+c.target)}&mt=1`;
    const res=await fetch(url,{cache:'no-store'});
    if(!res.ok)throw new Error('translation');
    const data=await res.json();
    const translated=decodeTranslationText(data?.responseData?.translatedText||'').trim();
    if(!translated||Number(data?.responseStatus||200)>=400)throw new Error('translation');
    output.value=translated;
    status.textContent='تمت الترجمة.';
  }catch(err){
    console.error(err);
    status.classList.add('error');
    status.textContent='تعذر الاتصال بخدمة الترجمة الآن. حاول مرة أخرى.';
  }
}

function compressImageFile(file,maxWidth=1200,maxHeight=800,quality=.82){return new Promise((resolve,reject)=>{if(!file?.type?.startsWith('image/')){reject(new Error('not image'));return}const reader=new FileReader();reader.onload=()=>{const img=new Image();img.onload=()=>{let w=img.naturalWidth,h=img.naturalHeight,scale=Math.min(1,maxWidth/w,maxHeight/h);w=Math.max(1,Math.round(w*scale));h=Math.max(1,Math.round(h*scale));const canvas=document.createElement('canvas');canvas.width=w;canvas.height=h;const ctx=canvas.getContext('2d');ctx.drawImage(img,0,0,w,h);resolve(canvas.toDataURL('image/jpeg',quality))};img.onerror=reject;img.src=reader.result};reader.onerror=reject;reader.readAsDataURL(file)})}

}

async function fetchSeasonalWeather(){
 const url='https://seasonal-api.open-meteo.com/v1/seasonal?latitude=37.5665&longitude=126.9780&daily=temperature_2m_max,temperature_2m_min,precipitation_sum,cloud_cover_mean,relative_humidity_2m_mean&timezone=Asia%2FSeoul&start_date=2026-11-10&end_date=2026-11-15&models=ecmwf_seas5_ensemble_mean';
 const res=await fetch(url,{cache:'no-store'});if(!res.ok)throw new Error('seasonal unavailable');
 const data=await res.json(),d=data.daily;if(!d?.time?.length)throw new Error('seasonal empty');
 return {source:'seasonal',updatedAt:new Date().toISOString(),days:d.time.map((date,i)=>{const rain=Number(d.precipitation_sum?.[i]||0),cloud=Number(d.cloud_cover_mean?.[i]||0);const [icon,description]=weatherFromSeasonal(cloud,rain);return {date,icon,description,max:Number(d.temperature_2m_max[i]),min:Number(d.temperature_2m_min[i]),rainLabel:rain<0.1?'لا أمطار ظاهرة':`أمطار متوقعة ${rain.toFixed(1)} ملم`}})}
}
async function loadLiveWeather(){
 const host=document.getElementById('liveWeatherDays');if(!host)return;
 document.getElementById('liveWeatherHeading').textContent='توقعات الطقس خلال الرحلة';
 const cached=readCachedWeather();
 if(!renderWeatherData(cached))renderWeatherLoading();
 try{
  let payload;
  try{payload=await fetchOperationalWeather()}catch(e){payload=await fetchSeasonalWeather()}
  cacheWeather(payload);renderWeatherData(payload)
 }catch(e){
  if(!cached){
   host.innerHTML='<div class="weather-loading">تعذر الاتصال بمصدر الطقس الآن. أعد فتح الصفحة عند توفر الإنترنت.</div>';
   document.getElementById('liveWeatherSubtitle').textContent='10–15 نوفمبر 2026'
  }
 }
}
renderAll();document.querySelectorAll('.latin-number').forEach(el=>{el.value=cleanLatinNumber(el.value);el.addEventListener('input',()=>{const start=el.selectionStart;el.value=cleanLatinNumber(el.value);try{el.setSelectionRange(start,start)}catch(e){}})});loadCurrencyRate();updateTranslationLabels();loadLiveWeather();setInterval(loadLiveWeather,21600000);

function privateClone(value){return JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(value))}
function newPrivateId(prefix){return `${prefix}-${Date.now()}-${Math.random().toString(36).slice(2,8)}`}
function todayRiyadh(){
  const parts=new Intl.DateTimeFormat("en-CA",{timeZone:"Asia/Riyadh",year:"numeric",month:"2-digit",day:"2-digit"}).formatToParts(new Date());
  const get=type=>parts.find(p=>p.type===type)?.value||"";
  return `${get("year")}-${get("month")}-${get("day")}`
}
function isoAfterDays(days){
  const d=new Date(`${todayRiyadh()}T12:00:00+03:00`);
  d.setDate(d.getDate()+days);
  return d.toISOString().slice(0,10)
}
function formatPrivateDate(value){
  if(!value)return "—";
  try{return new Intl.DateTimeFormat("ar-SA-u-nu-latn-ca-gregory",{day:"numeric",month:"short",year:"numeric",timeZone:"Asia/Riyadh"}).format(new Date(`${value}T12:00:00+03:00`))}
  catch(_e){return value}
}

  function closeLoginUI(){
    if(!backdrop||!login)return;
    backdrop.classList.remove('open');
    backdrop.dataset.mode='';
    login.hidden=true;
    login.setAttribute('aria-hidden','true');
    if(editor)editor.hidden=false;
  }
  window.__openAdminLoginUI=openLoginUI;
  window.__closeAdminLoginUI=closeLoginUI;
  button?.addEventListener('click',()=>{if(!document.body.classList.contains('admin-mode'))openLoginUI()});
  closeButton?.addEventListener('click',closeLoginUI);
  backdrop?.addEventListener('click',e=>{if(e.target===backdrop&&backdrop.dataset.mode==='admin')closeLoginUI()});
})();

let state=load();state.program=safeArray(state.program);state.suggestions=safeArray(state.suggestions);state.flights=safeArray(state.flights);state.lodging=safeArray(state.lodging);state.highlights=safeArray(state.highlights);state.exhibitors=safeArray(state.exhibitors);state.sponsors=safeArray(state.sponsors);
function publicPayload(){return {program:safeArray(state.program),suggestions:safeArray(state.suggestions),flights:safeArray(state.flights),lodging:safeArray(state.lodging),highlights:safeArray(state.highlights),exhibitors:safeArray(state.exhibitors),updatedAt:serverTimestamp()}}
async function persistToFirebase(){if(!isAdmin)return;try{await Promise.all([set(PUBLIC_REF,publicPayload()),set(SPONSORS_REF,safeArray(state.sponsors))]);toast("تم حفظ التحديث")}catch(err){console.error(err);toast("تعذر حفظ التحديث في السحابة")}}
function save(){localStorage.setItem(STORAGE_KEY,JSON.stringify(state));if(isAdmin){clearTimeout(firebaseSaveTimer);firebaseSaveTimer=setTimeout(persistToFirebase,260)}}
function toast(msg){let t=document.querySelector('.status-toast');if(!t){t=document.createElement('div');t.className='status-toast';document.body.appendChild(t)}t.textContent=msg;t.classList.add('show');setTimeout(()=>t.classList.remove('show'),2200)}

function renderLegend(){const el=document.getElementById('programLegend');if(!el)return;const items=['',...PROGRAM_FILTER_CATEGORIES];el.innerHTML=items.map(c=>{const isAll=!c;const active=c===activeProgramCategory;const icon=isAll?`<span class="filter-all-icon" aria-hidden="true"><b></b><b></b><b></b><b></b></span>`:`<i style="--dot:${CATEGORY_COLORS[c]||'#708090'}"></i>`;return `<button type="button" class="program-filter-btn ${isAll?'filter-all':''} ${active?'active':''}" data-program-filter="${esc(c)}" aria-pressed="${active?'true':'false'}" aria-label="${isAll?'عرض جميع الأنشطة':`عرض تصنيف ${esc(c)}`}">${icon}<span class="filter-label">${isAll?'الكل':esc(c)}</span></button>`}).join('');el.querySelectorAll('[data-program-filter]').forEach(btn=>btn.addEventListener('click',e=>{e.preventDefault();activeProgramCategory=btn.dataset.programFilter||'';renderLegend();renderProgram()}))}
function normalizeClock(value){const match=String(value||'').trim().match(/^(\d{1,2}):(\d{2})$/);if(!match)return'';const h=Number(match[1]),m=Number(match[2]);if(h<0||h>23||m<0||m>59)return'';return `${String(h).padStart(2,'0')}:${String(m).padStart(2,'0')}`}
function clockRank(value){const normalized=normalizeClock(value);if(!normalized)return Number.POSITIVE_INFINITY;const [h,m]=normalized.split(':').map(Number);return h*60+m}

function normalizePublic(remote){const base=clone(DEFAULT_STATE);return {program:Array.isArray(remote?.program)?remote.program:base.program,suggestions:Array.isArray(remote?.suggestions)?remote.suggestions:base.suggestions,flights:Array.isArray(remote?.flights)?remote.flights:base.flights,lodging:Array.isArray(remote?.lodging)?remote.lodging:base.lodging,highlights:Array.isArray(remote?.highlights)?remote.highlights:base.highlights,exhibitors:Array.isArray(remote?.exhibitors)?remote.exhibitors:base.exhibitors}}
function startPublicSync(){onValue(PUBLIC_REF,snapshot=>{const remote=snapshot.val();if(!remote){updateLastUpdated(null);return}const clean=normalizePublic(remote);state={...state,...clean,sponsors:safeArray(state.sponsors)};localStorage.setItem(STORAGE_KEY,JSON.stringify(state));updateLastUpdated(remote.updatedAt);renderAll()},err=>{console.error(err);toast("تعذر تحميل آخر تحديث")})}
function startSponsorsSync(){if(sponsorsUnsubscribe){sponsorsUnsubscribe();sponsorsUnsubscribe=null}if(!isAdmin)return;sponsorsUnsubscribe=onValue(SPONSORS_REF,snapshot=>{if(snapshot.exists()){state.sponsors=safeArray(snapshot.val());localStorage.setItem(STORAGE_KEY,JSON.stringify(state));renderSponsors()}else{set(SPONSORS_REF,safeArray(state.sponsors)).catch(console.error)}},err=>console.error(err))}

}

function publicPayload(){return {program:safeArray(state.program),suggestions:safeArray(state.suggestions),flights:safeArray(state.flights),lodging:safeArray(state.lodging),highlights:safeArray(state.highlights),exhibitors:safeArray(state.exhibitors),updatedAt:serverTimestamp()}}
async function persistToFirebase(){if(!isAdmin)return;try{await Promise.all([set(PUBLIC_REF,publicPayload()),set(SPONSORS_REF,safeArray(state.sponsors))]);toast("تم حفظ التحديث")}catch(err){console.error(err);toast("تعذر حفظ التحديث في السحابة")}}

function toast(msg){let t=document.querySelector('.status-toast');if(!t){t=document.createElement('div');t.className='status-toast';document.body.appendChild(t)}t.textContent=msg;t.classList.add('show');setTimeout(()=>t.classList.remove('show'),2200)}
let activeProgramCategory='';
const PROGRAM_FILTER_CATEGORIES=CATEGORIES.filter(c=>c!=='أخرى');
function renderLegend(){const el=document.getElementById('programLegend');if(!el)return;const items=['',...PROGRAM_FILTER_CATEGORIES];el.innerHTML=items.map(c=>{const isAll=!c;const active=c===activeProgramCategory;const icon=isAll?`<span class="filter-all-icon" aria-hidden="true"><b></b><b></b><b></b><b></b></span>`:`<i style="--dot:${CATEGORY_COLORS[c]||'#708090'}"></i>`;return `<button type="button" class="program-filter-btn ${isAll?'filter-all':''} ${active?'active':''}" data-program-filter="${esc(c)}" aria-pressed="${active?'true':'false'}" aria-label="${isAll?'عرض جميع الأنشطة':`عرض تصنيف ${esc(c)}`}">${icon}<span class="filter-label">${isAll?'الكل':esc(c)}</span></button>`}).join('');el.querySelectorAll('[data-program-filter]').forEach(btn=>btn.addEventListener('click',e=>{e.preventDefault();activeProgramCategory=btn.dataset.programFilter||'';renderLegend();renderProgram()}))}
function normalizeClock(value){const match=String(value||'').trim().match(/^(\d{1,2}):(\d{2})$/);if(!match)return'';const h=Number(match[1]),m=Number(match[2]);if(h<0||h>23||m<0||m>59)return'';return `${String(h).padStart(2,'0')}:${String(m).padStart(2,'0')}`}
function clockRank(value){const normalized=normalizeClock(value);if(!normalized)return Number.POSITIVE_INFINITY;const [h,m]=normalized.split(':').map(Number);return h*60+m}
function timeOrderCompare(a,b){const diff=clockRank(a.r.time)-clockRank(b.r.time);return Number.isNaN(diff)||diff===0?a.i-b.i:diff}
function getOrderedProgramRows(day){const rows=state.program.map((r,i)=>({r,i})).filter(x=>x.r.date===day);const allOrdered=rows.length>0&&rows.every(x=>Number.isFinite(Number(x.r.sortOrder)));rows.sort(allOrdered?(a,b)=>Number(a.r.sortOrder)-Number(b.r.sortOrder)||a.i-b.i:timeOrderCompare);return rows}
function resequenceProgramDay(day,orderedIndices){orderedIndices.forEach((index,position)=>{if(state.program[index]&&state.program[index].date===day)state.program[index].sortOrder=position})}
function sortProgramDayByTime(day){if(!day)return;const rows=state.program.map((r,i)=>({r,i})).filter(x=>x.r.date===day).sort(timeOrderCompare);resequenceProgramDay(day,rows.map(x=>x.i))}
function programDragHandle(i,day){return isAdmin?`<button class="row-drag-handle" type="button" draggable="true" data-
rn {program:safeArray(state.program),suggestions:safeArray(state.suggestions),flights:safeArray(state.flights),lodging:safeArray(state.lodging),highlights:safeArray(state.highlights),exhibitors:safeArray(state.exhibitors),updatedAt:serverTimestamp()}}

async function persistFollowups(){
  if(!isAdmin)return;
  try{await set(FOLLOWUPS_REF,{items:followupTasks,updatedAt:serverTimestamp()});toast("تم حفظ المتابعات")}
  catch(err){console.error(err);toast("تعذر حفظ المتابعات")}
}
async function persistFinance(){
  if(!isAdmin)return;
  try{await set(FINANCE_REF,{params:financeState.params,costs:financeState.costs,updatedAt:serverTimestamp()});financeUpdatedAt=Date.now();updateFinanceSaveText();toast("تم حفظ المالية")}
  catch(err){console.error(err);toast("تعذر حفظ المالية")}
}
function queueFinanceSave(){
  clearTimeout(financeSaveTimer);
  const label=document.getElementById("financeLastSave");
  if(label)label.textContent="جاري الحفظ…";
  financeSaveTimer=setTimeout(persistFinance,320)
}
function updateFinanceSaveText(){
  const label=document.getElementById("financeLastSave");
  if(label)label.textContent=financeUpdatedAt?`آخر حفظ: ${formatPrivateDateTime(financeUpdatedAt)}`:"جاهز للحفظ"
}
function followupHistoryHtml(task,limit=4){
  const items=[...task.history].sort((a,b)=>String(b.date).localeCompare(String(a.date))).slice(0,limit);
  if(!items.length)return '<span class="muted">يبدأ السجل مع أول تحديث.</span>';
  return items.map(h=>`<div class="followup-history-item"><time>${esc(formatPrivateDate(h.date))}</time><span><strong>${esc(FOLLOWUP_EVENT_LABELS[h.type]||"تحديث")}:</strong> ${esc(h.note||"تم التحديث")}</span></div>`).join("")
}
function followupMatchesDue(task,filter){
  if(!filter)return true;
  if(filter==="open")return isOpenFollowup(task);
  const kind=followupDueKind(task);
  if(filter==="today"||filter==="overdue")return kind===filter;
  if(filter==="next7"){
    const end=isoAfterDays(7);
    return isOpenFollowup(task)&&Boolean(task.nextActionDate)&&task.nextActionDate>=todayRiyadh()&&task.nextActionDate<=end
  }
  return true
}
function renderFollowupDashboard(){
  const open=followupTasks.filter(isOpenFollowup).length;
  const today=followupTasks.filter(t=>followupDueKind(t)==="today").length;
  const overdue=followupTasks.filter(t=>followupDueKind(t)==="overdue").length;
  const waiting=followupTasks.filter(t=>t.status==="waiting").length;
  const completed=followupTasks.filter(t=>t.status==="completed").length;
  const values={followupOpenCount:open,followupTodayCount:today,followupOverdueCount:overdue,followupWaitingCount:waiting,followupCompletedCount:completed};
  Object.entries(values).forEach(([id,value])=>{const el=document.getElementById(id);if(el)el.textContent=String(value)});
  document.querySelectorAll("#followupDashboard .followup-stat").forEach(btn=>{
    btn.classList.toggle("active",(btn.dataset.dueFilter&&btn.dataset.dueFilter===followupFilters.due)||(btn.dataset.statusFilter&&btn.dataset.statusFilter===followupFilters.status))
  })
}

  if(!body||!isAdmi

  document.getElementById("deleteFollowupTask").hidden=!task;
  const history=document.getElementById("followupTaskHistory");
  history.innerHTML=task?`<h4>سجل التواصل</h4><div class="followup-history-list">${followupHistoryHtml(task,12)}</div>`:"";
  document.getElementById("followupTaskModal").hidden=false;
  setTimeout(()=>document.getElementById("fuEntity")?.focus(),50)
}
function closeFollowupTaskModal(){document.getElementById("followupTaskModal").hidden=true;followupEditId=null}
async function saveFollowupTaskFromModal(){
  const entity=document.getElementById("fuEntity").value.trim();
  const subject=document.getElementById("fuSubject").value.trim();
  if(!entity||!subject){toast("اكتب الجهة والموضوع");return}
  const existing=followupEditId?followupTasks.find(t=>t.id===followupEditId):null;
  const now=new Date().toISOString();
  const task=normalizeFollowupTask({
    ...existing,
    id:existing?.id||newPrivateId("followup"),
    entity,subject,
    contactName:document.getElementById("fuContactName").value.trim(),
    contactValue:document.getElementById("fuEmail").value.trim(),
    channel:document.getElementById("fuChannel").value,
    status:document.getElementById("fuStatus").value,
    priority:document.getElementById("fuPriority").value,
    owner:document.getElementById("fuOwner").value.trim(),
    lastActionDate:document.getElementById("fuLastAction").value,
    nextActionDate:document.getElementById("fuNextAction").value,
    notes:document.getElementById("fuNotes").value.trim(),
    history:existing?.history||[{id:newPrivateId("log"),type:"created",date:todayRiyadh(),note:"تم إنشاء المهمة"}],
    createdAt:existing?.createdAt||now,
    updatedAt:now
  });
  if(existing)followupTasks=followupTasks.map(t=>t.id===task.id?task:t);
  else followupTasks.push(task);
  renderFollowups();
  closeFollowupTaskModal();
  await persistFollowups()
}
async function deleteFollowupTaskFromModal(){
  if(!followupEditId)return;
  const task=followupTasks.find(t=>t.id===followupEditId);
  if(!confirm(`حذف مهمة ${task?.entity||""}؟`))return;
  followupTasks=followupTasks.filter(t=>t.id!==followupEditId);
  closeFollowupTaskModal();
  renderFollowups();
  await persistFollowups()
}
function openFollowupLogModal(id){
  const task=followupTasks.find(t=>t.id===id);
  if(!task)return;
  followupLogId=id;
  document.getElementById("followupLogModalTitle").textContent=`تحديث التواصل: ${task.entity}`;
  document.getElementById("fuLogType").value="followup";
  document.getElementById("fuLogDate").value=todayRiyadh();
  document.getElementById("fuLogNext").value=task.nextActionDate||isoAfterDays(3);
  document.getElementById("fuLogNote").value="";
  document.getElementById("followupLogModal").hidden=false;
  setTimeout(()=>document.getElementById("fuLogNote")?.focus(),50)
}
function closeFollowupLogModal(){document.getElementById("followupLogModal").hidden=true;followupLogId=null}

  const date=document.getElementById("fuLogDate").value;
  const next=document.getElementById("fuLogNext").value;
  const note=document.getElementById("fuLogNote").value.trim();
  if(!date||!note){toast("أدخل تاريخ التحديث وتفاصيله");return}
  const statusByEvent={sent:"waiting",followup:"waiting",reply_received:"replied",response_sent:"waiting",call:"in_progress",meeting:"in_progress",completed:"completed"};
  const updated=n
sk.entity}`;

  document.getElementById("fuLogDate").value=todayRiyadh();
  document.getElementById("fuLogNext").value=task.nextActionDate||isoAfterDays(3);
  document.getElementById("fuLogNote").value="";
  document.getElementById("followupLogModal").hidden=false;
  setTimeout(()=>document.getElementById("fuLogNote")?.focus(),50)
}
function closeFollowupLogModal(){document.getElementById("followupLogModal").hidden=true;followupLogId=null}
async function saveFollowupLogFromModal(){
  const task=followupTasks.find(t=>t.id===followupLogId);
  if(!task)return;
  const type=document.getElementById("fuLogType").value;
  const date=document.getElementById("fuLogDate").value;
  const next=document.getElementById("fuLogNext").value;
  const note=document.getElementById("fuLogNote").value.trim();
  if(!date||!note){toast("أدخل تاريخ التحديث وتفاصيله");return}
  const statusByEvent={sent:"waiting",followup:"waiting",reply_received:"replied",response_sent:"waiting",call:"in_progress",meeting:"in_progress",completed:"completed"};
  const updated=normalizeFollowupTask({
    ...task,
    status:statusByEvent[type]||task.status,
    lastActionDate:type==="note"?task.lastActionDate:date,
    nextActionDate:type==="completed"?"":next,
    history:[...task.history,{id:newPrivateId("log"),type,date,note}],
    updatedAt:new Date().toISOString()
  });
  followupTasks=followupTasks.map(t=>t.id===updated.id?updated:t);
  closeFollowupLogModal();
  renderFollowups();
  await persistFollowups()
}
function exportFollowupsCsv(){
  const headings=["الجهة","الموضوع","اسم مسؤول التواصل","بيانات التواصل","الوسيلة","المرحلة","آخر تواصل","المتابعة القادمة","الأولوية","المسؤول","الملاحظات"];
  const clean=value=>`"${String(value??"").replaceAll('"','""')}"`;
  const lines=[headings.map(clean).join(","),...followupTasks.map(t=>[
    t.entity,t.subject,t.contactName,t.contactValue,FOLLOWUP_CHANNEL_LABELS[t.channel],FOLLOWUP_STATUS_LABELS[t.status],t.lastActionDate,t.nextActionDate,FOLLOWUP_PRIORITY_LABELS[t.priority],t.owner,t.notes
  ].map(clean).join(","))];
  const blob=new Blob(["\uFEFF"+lines.join("\r\n")],{type:"text/csv;charset=utf-8"});
  const a=document.createElement("a");a.href=URL.createObjectURL(blob);a.download="متابعة-التواصل-رحلة-سيئول-2026.csv";a.click();setTimeout(()=>URL.revokeObjectURL(a.href),1000)
}
function financeNumber(value){return Number(value||0)}
function financePeople(){return financeNumber(financeState.params.payingParticipants)+financeNumber(financeState.params.complimentaryParticipants)}
function financeQuantity(mode){
  if(mode==="perPerson")return financePeople();
  if(mode==="perPaying")return financeNumber(financeState.params.payingParticipants);
  if(mode==="perPersonNight")return financePeople()*financeNumber(financeState.params.nights);
  if(mode==="perPersonDay")return financePeople()*financeNumber(financeState.params.programDays);
  return 1
}
function financeRows(){return financeState.costs.map((row,index)=>({...row,index,quantity:financeQuantity(row.mode),total:financeQuantity(row.mode)*financeNumber(row.unit)}))}
function financeFormat(value){return new Intl.NumberFormat("en-US",{maximumFractionDigits:0}).format(Math.round(financeNumber(value)))}
function financeMoney(value){return `<span class="sar-amount"><span class="sar-symbol" aria-hidden="true"></span><span>${financeFormat(value)}</span></span>`}

  const rows=financeRows();
  const costs=rows.reduce((sum,row)=>sum+row.tota

  document.getElementById("finCostDetails").value=row?.details||"";
  document.getElementById("finCostMode").value=row?.mode||"group";
  document.getElementById("finCostUnit").value=row?.unit||0;
  document.getElementById("deleteFinanceCost").hidden=!row;
  document.getElementById("financeCostModal").hidden=false;
  setTimeout(()=>document.getElementById("finCostName")?.focus(),50)
}
function closeFinanceCostModal(){document.getElementById("financeCostModal").hidden=true;financeEditIndex=null}
async function saveFinanceCostFromModal(){
  const name=document.getElementById("finCostName").value.trim();
  if(!name){toast("اكتب اسم بند التكلفة");return}
  const previous=financeEditIndex===null?null:financeState.costs[financeEditIndex];
  const row={
    id:previous?.id||newPrivateId("cost"),
    name,
    details:document.getElementById("finCostDetails").value.trim(),
    mode:document.getElementById("finCostMode").value,
    unit:financeNumber(document.getElementById("finCostUnit").value),
    linked:Boolean(previous?.linked)
  };
  if(financeEditIndex===null)financeState.costs.push(row);else financeState.costs[financeEditIndex]=row;
  closeFinanceCostModal();renderFinance();await persistFinance()
}
async function deleteFinanceCostFromModal(){
  if(financeEditIndex===null)return;
  if(!confirm(`حذف بند ${financeState.costs[financeEditIndex]?.name||""}؟`))return;
  financeState.costs.splice(financeEditIndex,1);
  closeFinanceCostModal();renderFinance();await persistFinance()
}
function financeReportData(){
  const p=financeState.params,rows=financeRows(),costs=rows.reduce((sum,row)=>sum+row.total,0);
  const revenue=financeNumber(p.payingParticipants)*financeNumber(p.participantPrice)+financeNumber(p.sponsorshipIncome);
  const net=revenue-costs;
  const breakEven=financeNumber(p.payingParticipants)?Math.max(0,(costs-financeNumber(p.sponsorshipIncome))/financeNumber(p.payingParticipants)):0;
  return {p,rows,costs,revenue,net,breakEven}
}

  })
}

let sarToKrwRate=null,currencyDirection='sar-krw';
function setCurrencyStatus(message,error=false){const el=document.getElementById('currencyStatus');if(el){el.textContent=message;el.classList.toggle('error',error)}}
function latinDigits(value){return String(value??'').replace(/[٠-٩]/g,d=>'0123456789'['٠١٢٣٤٥٦٧٨٩'.indexOf(d)]).replace(/[۰-۹]/g,d=>'0123456789'['۰۱۲۳۴۵۶۷۸۹'.indexOf(d)])}
function cleanLatinNumber(value){let v=latinDigits(value).replace(/[^0-9.]/g,'');const first=v.indexOf('.');if(first>=0)v=v.slice(0,first+1)+v.slice(first+1).replace(/\./g,'');return v}
function currencyValue(id){const el=document.getElementById(id);if(!el)return 0;const clean=cleanLatinNumber(el.value);if(el.value!==clean)el.value=clean;return Number(clean||0)}
function convertFromSar(){if(!sarToKrwRate)return;const sar=currencyValue('currencySar'),out=document.getElementById('currencyKrw');if(out)out.value=String(Math.round(sar*sarToKrwRate))}
function convertFromKrw(){if(!sarToKrwRate)return;const krw=currencyValue('currencyKrw'),out=document.getElementById('currencySar');if(out)out.value=(krw/sarToKrwRate).toFixed(2)}
async function loadCurrencyRate(){
  const status=document.getElementById('currencyStatus');
  try{
    if(status){status.classList.remove('error');status.textContent='جاري تحديث سعر الصرف…'}
    let rate=0,date='';
    let res=await fetch('https://api.frankfurter.dev/v2/rate/SAR/KRW',{cache:'no-store'});
    if(res.ok){
      const data=await res.json();
      rate=Number(data?.rate);
      date=data?.date||'';
    }
    if(!rate){
      res=await fetch('https://open.er-api.com/v6/latest/SAR',{cache:'no-store'});
      if(!res.ok)throw new Error('rate');
      const data=await res.json();
      rate=Number(data?.rates?.KRW);
      date=data?.time_last_update_utc||'';
    }
    if(!rate)throw new Error('rate');
    sarToKrwRate=rate;
    convertFromSar();
    const shownRate=new Intl.NumberFormat('en-US',{maximumFractionDigits:2}).format(rate);
    setCurrencyStatus(`1 ريال سعودي = ${shownRate} وون كوري${date?' • آخر تحديث: '+date:''}`);
  }catch(err){
    console.error(err);
    setCurrencyStatus('تعذر تحديث سعر الصرف الآن. تحقق من الاتصال وحاول مرة أخرى.',true);
  }
}
let translationDirection='ar-ko';
function translationCodes(){return translationDirection==='ar-ko'?{source:'ar',target:'ko',sourceLabel:'العربية',targetLabel:'الكورية'}:{source:'ko',target:'ar',sourceLabel:'الكورية',targetLabel:'العربية'}}
function updateTranslationLabels(){const c=translationCodes();document.getElementById('translationSourceLabel').textContent=c.sourceLabel;document.getEl
.value='';
    const url=`https://api.mymemory.translated.net/get?q=${encodeURIComponent(textValue)}&langpair=${encodeURIComponent(c.source+'|'+c.target)}&mt=1`;
    const res=await fetch(url,{cache:'no-store'});
    if(!res.ok)throw new Error('translation');
    const data=await res.json();
    const translated=decodeTranslationText(data?.responseData?.translatedText||'').trim();
    if(!translated||Number(data?.responseStatus||200)>=400)throw new Error('translation');
    output.value=translated;
    status.textContent='تمت الترجمة.';
  }catch(err){
    console.error(err);
    status.classList.add('error');
    status.textContent='تعذر الاتصال بخدمة الترجمة الآن. حاول مرة أخرى.';
  }
}

function compressImageFile(file,maxWidth=1200,maxHeight=800,quality=.82){return new Promise((resolve,reject)=>{if(!file?.type?.startsWith('image/')){reject(new Error('not image'));return}const reader=new FileReader();reader.onload=()=>{const img=new Image();img.onload=()=>{let w=img.naturalWidth,h=img.naturalHeight,scale=Math.min(1,maxWidth/w,maxHeight/h);w=Math.max(1,Math.round(w*scale));h=Math.max(1,Math.round(h*scale));const canvas=document.createElement('canvas');canvas.width=w;canvas.height=h;const ctx=canvas.getContext('2d');ctx.drawImage(img,0,0,w,h);resolve(canvas.toDataURL('image/jpeg',quality))};img.onerror=reject;img.src=reader.result};reader.onerror=reject;reader.readAsDataURL(file)})}

function canvasLines(ctx,text,maxWidth,maxLines=20){const clean=String(text||'').replace(/\s+/g,' ').trim();if(!clean)return[];const words=clean.split(' '),lines=[];let line='';for(const word of words){const test=line?line+' '+word:word;if(ctx.measureText(test).width<=maxWidth||!line)line=test;else{lines.push(line);line=word;if(lines.length>=maxLines-1)break}}if(line&&lines.length<maxLines)lines.push(line);if(lines.length===maxLines&&words.join(' ').length>lines.join(' ').length){let last=lines[maxLines-1];while(last.length>1&&ctx.measureText(last+'…').width>maxWidth)last=last.slice(0,-1);lines[maxLines-1]=last+'…'}return lines}

  renderAll()
}

async function submitAdminLogin(){
  const pin=adminPinInput.value.trim();
  if(pin.length!==6){adminLoginError.textContent="أدخل رمز الإدارة المكون من 6 أرقام";return}
  const submit=document.getElementById("adminLoginSubmit");
  submit.disabled=true;submit.textContent="جاري الدخول…";adminLoginError.textContent="";
  try{
    await authInit;
    const credential=await signInWithEmailAndPassword(auth,ADMIN_EMAIL,pin);
    if(credential.user.uid!==ADMIN_UID){await signOut(auth);throw new Error("unauthorized")}
    isAdmin=true;
    applyAdminMode();
    startSponsorsSync();
    startPrivateToolsSync();
    await seedFirebaseIfEmpty();
    closeAdminLogin();
    toast("تم فتح وضع الإدارة")
  }catch(err){
    console.error(err);isAdmin=false;applyAdminMode();adminLoginError.textContent="رمز الإدارة غير صحيح"
  }finally{submit.disabled=false;submit.textContent="دخول"}
}

});

function normalizeClock(value){const match=String(value||'').trim().match(/^(\d{1,2}):(\d{2})$/);if(!match)return'';const h=Number(match[1]),m=Number(match[2]);if(h<0||h>23||m<0||m>59)return'';return `${String(h).padStart(2,'0')}:${String(m).padStart(2,'0')}`}
function clockRank(value){const normalized=normalizeClock(value);if(!normalized)return Number.POSITIVE_INFINITY;const [h,m]=normalized.split(':').map(Number);return h*60+m}
function timeOrderCompare(a,b){const diff=clockRank(a.r.time)-clockRank(b.r.time);return Number.isNaN(diff)||diff===0?a.i-b.i:diff}
function getOrderedProgramRows(day){const rows=state.program.map((r,i)=>({r,i})).filter(x=>x.r.date===day);const allOrdered=rows.length>0&&rows.every(x=>Number.isFinite(Number(x.r.sortOrder)));rows.sort(allOrdered?(a,b)=>Number(a.r.sortOrder)-Number(b.r.sortOrder)||a.i-b.i:timeOrderCompare);return rows}
function resequenceProgramDay(day,orderedIndices){orderedIndices.forEach((index,position)=>{if(state.program[index]&&state.program[index].date===day)state.program[index].sortOrder=position})}
function sortProgramDayByTime(day){if(!day)return;const rows=state.program.map((r,i)=>({r,i})).filter(x=>x.r.date===day).sort(timeOrderCompare);resequenceProgramDay(day,rows.map(x=>x.i))}
function programDragHandle(i,day){return isAdmin?`<button class="row-drag-handle" type="button" draggable="true" data-program-drag="${i}" data-drag-day="${esc(day)}" title="اسحب لرفع الصف أو إنزاله" aria-label="اسحب لإعادة ترتيب الصف"><svg aria-hidden="true" viewBox="0 0 18 24"><circle cx="5" cy="5" r="1.6"></circle><circle cx="13" cy="5" r="1.6"></circle><circle cx="5" cy="12" r="1.6"></circle><circle cx="13" cy="12" r="1.6"></circle><circle cx="5" cy="19" r="1.6"></circle><circle cx="13" cy="19" r="1.6"></circle></svg></button>`:''}
function initProgramDragAndDrop(host){let dragIndex=null,dragDay='',dragRow=null;host.querySelectorAll('[data-program-drag]').forEach(handle=>{handle.addEventListener('click',e=>{e.preventDefault();e.stopPropagation()});handle.addEventListener('pointerdown',e=>e.stopPropagation());handle.addEventListener('dragstart',e=>{dragIndex=Number(handle.dataset.programDrag);dragDay=handle.dataset.dragDay||'';dragRow=handle.closest('.event-row');if(!dragRow||!Number.isInteger(dragIndex)){e.preventDefault();return}e.dataTransfer.effectAllowed='move';e.dataTransfer.setData('text/plain',String(dragIndex));requestAnimationFrame(()=>dragRow?.classList.add('is-dragging'))});handle.addEventListener('dragend',()=>{host.querySelectorAll('.event-row').forEach(row=>row.classList.remove('is-dragging'));host.querySelectorAll('.events').forEach(box=>box.classList.remove('is-drag-over'));dragIndex=null;dragDay='';dragRow=null})});host.querySelectorAll('.events[data-day]').forEach(container=>{container.addEventListener('dragover',e=>{if(dragIndex===null||container.dataset.day!==dragDay)return;e.preventDefault();e.dataTransfer.dropEffect='move';container.classList.add('is-drag-over');const rows=[...container.querySelectorAll('.event-row:not(.is-dragging)')];const target=rows.find(row=>e.clientY<row.getBoundingClientRect().top+row.getBoundingClientRect().height/2);if(target)container.insertBefore(dragRow,target);else container.appendChild(dragRow)});container.addEventListener('dragleave',e=>{if(!container.contains(e.relatedTarget))container.classList.remove('is-drag-over')});container.addEventListener('drop',e=>{if(dragIndex===null||container.dataset.day!==dragDay)return;e.preventDefault();container.cl
sertBefore(dragRow,target);else container.appendChild(dragRow)});container.addEventListener('dragleave',e=>{if(!container.contains(e.relatedTarget))container.classList.remove('is-drag-over')});container.addEventListener('drop',e=>{if(dragIndex===null||container.dataset.day!==dragDay)return;e.preventDefault();container.classList.remove('is-drag-over');const visibleOrder=[...container.querySelectorAll('.event-row')].map(row=>Number(row.dataset.index)).filter(Number.isInteger);const visibleSet=new Set(visibleOrder);let cursor=0;const fullOrder=getOrderedProgramRows(dragDay).map(x=>x.i).map(index=>visibleSet.has(index)?visibleOrder[cursor++]:index);resequenceProgramDay(dragDay,fullOrder);save();renderProgram();toast('تم حفظ ترتيب صفوف اليوم')})})}
function renderProgram(){const host=document.getElementById('programDays');if(!host)return;const dayCards=DAYS.map(day=>{const rows=getOrderedProgramRows(day).filter(x=>!activeProgramCategory||x.r.category===activeProgramCategory);if(activeProgramCategory&&!rows.length)return'';return `<div class="day-card"><div class="day-head"><h3>${day}</h3><div class="day-actions"><button type="button" class="day-share compact-service-btn" data-share-day="${day}" ${rows.length?'':'disabled'}>${actionIcon('share')}<span>مشاركة اليوم</span></button>${isAdmin?`<button class="add-btn compact-service-btn" data-add="program" data-date="${day}" type="button">${actionIcon('plus')}<span>إضافة بند</span></button>`:''}</div></div><div class="events" data-day="${esc(day)}">${rows.length?rows.map(({r,i})=>`<div class="event-row ${catClass(r.category)}" data-edit="program" data-index="${i}" data-program-day="${esc(day)}"><span class="cat-chip">${esc(r.category)}</span><span class="event-time">${esc(r.time||'—')}</span><span class="event-title">${esc(r.title)}</span><span class="event-details">${esc(r.details)}</span><span class="event-place">${r.maps?`<a href="${esc(r.maps)}" target="_blank">${esc(r.place||'الموقع')}</a>`:esc(r.place||'')}</span>${programRowActions(i)}${programDragHandle(i,day)}</div>`).join(''):`<div class="muted empty-day">لم يُعتمد أي نشاط لهذا اليوم بعد.<br><small>اختر من جدول الأنشطة والزيارات المقترحة أو أضف بندًا مباشرة.</small></div>`}</div></div>`}).join('');host.innerHTML=dayCards||`<div class="muted empty-day" style="background:#fff;padding:28px;text-align:center;border-radius:18px">لا توجد أنشطة ضمن تصنيف «${esc(activeProgramCategory)}» في برنامج الرحلة.</div>`;host.querySelectorAll('[data-share-day]').forEach(btn=>btn.addEventListener('click',()=>shareProgramImage([btn.dataset.shareDay],`جمعية-ملاك-المطاعم-والمقاهي-برنامج-${btn.dataset.shareDay}-سيئول-2026.png`,activeProgramCategory)));if(isAdmin)initProgramDragAndDrop(host)}
function normalizedTitle(value){return String(value||'').trim().replace(/\s+/g,' ').toLowerCase()}
function deleteProgramItem(index){if(!isAdmin)return;if(!Number.isInteger(index)||!state.program[index])return;if(!confirm('حذف هذا البند من برنامج الرحلة؟'))return;state.program.splice(index,1);save();renderAll();toast('تم حذف البند من البرنامج')}
function deleteSuggestionItem(index){if(!isAdmin)return;if(!Number.isInteger(index)||!state.suggestions[index])return;const title=state.suggestions[index].title||'هذا المقترح';if(!confirm(`حذف «${title}» من الأنشطة والزيارات المقترحة؟\nلن يتأثر ما نُقل منه إلى برنامج الرحلة.`))return;state.suggestions.splice(index,1);save();renderAll();toast('تم حذف المقترح')}
function returnProgramItemToSuggestions(index){if(!isAdmin)return;if(!Number.isInteger(index)||!state.program[index])return;const row=state.program[index];const exists=state.suggestions.some(item=>normalizedTitle(item.title)===normalizedTitle(row.title)&&String(item.category||'')===String(row.category||''));if(!exists){state.suggestions.push({category:row.category||'نشاط معرفي',title:row.title||'',description:row.details||'',dates:row.date?[row.date]:[],time:row.time||'',place:row.place||row.title||'',site:'',maps:row.maps||'',owner:row.owner||'',status:row.status||'مقترح'})}state.program.splice(index,1);save();renderAll();toast(exists?'تمت إزالة البند من البرنامج وهو موجود في المقترحات':'تمت إعادة البند إلى المقترحات')}

    const res=await fetch(url,{cache:'no-store'});
    if(!res.ok)throw new Error('translation');
    const data=await res.json();

    if(!translated||Number(data?.responseStatus||200)>=400)throw new Error('translation');
    output.value=translated;

  }catch(err){

    status.classList.add('error');

  }

function defaultRow(type,prefill={}){const base={
 program:{category:'أخرى',date:DAYS[0],time:'',title:'',details:'',place:'',maps:'',owner:'',status:'مقترح'},
 suggestions:{category:'نشاط معرفي',title:'',description:'',dates:[],time:'',place:'',site:'',maps:'',owner:'',status:'مقترح'},
 flights:{origin:'',airline:'',departDate:'',firstFlight:'',hubArrival:'',seoulFlight:'',seoulArrival:'',notes:'',status:'مقترح'},
 lodging:{name:'',maps:'',reason:''},
 highlights:{titleAr:'',titleEn:'',category:'نشاط معرفي',summary:'',link:'',linkLabel:'عرض التفاصيل',image:''},
 exhibitors:{company:'',brand:'',product:'',category:'القهوة',country:'',hall:'',booth:'',site:'',owner:'',appointment:'',note:'',status:'للبحث'},
 sponsors:{entity:'',contact:'',interest:'',approach:'',status:''}
}[type];return {...base,...prefill}}
function compressImageFile(file,maxWidth=1200,maxHeight=800,quality=.82){return new Promise((resolve,reject)=>{if(!file?.type?.startsWith('image/')){reject(new Error('not image'));return}const reader=new FileReader();reader.onload=()=>{const img=new Image();img.onload=()=>{let w=img.naturalWidth,h=img.naturalHeight,scale=Math.min(1,maxWidth/w,maxHeight/h);w=Math.max(1,Math.round(w*scale));h=Math.max(1,Math.round(h*scale));const canvas=document.createElement('canvas');canvas.width=w;canvas.height=h;const ctx=canvas.getContext('2d');ctx.drawImage(img,0,0,w,h);resolve(canvas.toDataURL('image/jpeg',quality))};img.onerror=reject;img.src=reader.result};reader.onerror=reject;reader.readAsDataURL(file)})}
function bindModalImageFields(){fieldsHost.querySelectorAll('[data-image-editor]').forEach(box=>{const key=box.dataset.imageEditor,input=box.querySelector(`[data-modal-field="${key}"]`),file=box.querySelector('input[type="file"]'),preview=box.querySelector('.image-editor-preview');const draw=()=>{const src=(input.value||'').trim();preview.innerHTML=src?`<img src="${esc(src)}" alt="معاينة الصورة">`:'اختر صورة أو ألصق رابطها'};input.addEventListener('input',draw);file.addEventListener('change',async()=>{const selected=file.files?.[0];if(!selected)return;try{preview.textContent='جاري تجهيز الصورة…';input.value=await compressImageFile(selected);draw()}catch(err){console.error(err);preview.textContent='تعذر قراءة الصورة'}});draw()})}

function imageOrPlaceholder(src,label){return src?`<img src="${esc(src)}" alt="${esc(label)}" loading="lazy">`:`<div class="experience-placeholder">${esc(label||'صورة النشاط')}</div>`}
function renderHighlights(){const host=document.getElementById('highlightGrid');if(!host)return;host.innerHTML=state.highlights.map((r,i)=>`<article class="experience-card"><div class="admin-card-actions"><button data-edit="highlights" data-index="${i}" type="button">تعديل</button></div><div class="experience-visual">${imageOrPlaceholder(r.image,r.titleAr)}</div><div class="experience-body"><h3 class="experience-name">${esc(r.titleAr)}${r.titleEn?`<span class="experience-en">${esc(r.titleEn)}</span>`:''}</h3><p>${esc(r.summary)}</p>${r.link?`<a class="experience-link" href="${esc(r.link)}" rel="noopener" target="_blank">${esc(r.linkLabel||'عرض التفاصيل')}</a>`:''}</div></article>`).join('')||'<div class="experience-empty">ستظهر البطاقات بعد إضافتها من لوحة الإدارة.</div>'}

const EXHIBITOR_PREF_KEY='rcoa-seoul-exhibitor-prefs-v1';
function readExhibitorPrefs(){try{return JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(EXHIBITOR_PREF_KEY)||'{}')}catch(_e){return {}}}
let exhibitorPrefs=readExhibitorPrefs();
function saveExhibitorPrefs(){localStorage.setItem(EXHIBITOR_PREF_KEY,JSON.stringify(exhibitorPrefs))}
function exhibitorKey(r,i){return String(r.id||`${r.company}|${r.brand}|${r.booth}|${i}`)}
function renderExhibitorFilters(){const select=document.getElementById('exhibitorCategoryFilter');if(!select)return;const current=select.value,cats=[...new Set(state.exhibitors.map(x=>x.category).filter(Boolean))];select.innerHTML='<option value="">جميع الفئات</option>'+cats.map(c=>`<option value="${esc(c)}">${esc(c)}</option>`).join('');select.value=cats.includes(current)?current:''}
function renderExhibitors(){const host=document.getElementById('exhibitorGrid');if(!host)return;renderExhibitorFilters();const q=(document.getElementById('exhibitorSearch')?.value||'').trim().toLowerCase(),cat=document.getElementById('exhibitorCategoryFilter')?.value||'';const rows=state.exhibitors.map((r,i)=>({r,i,key:exhibitorKey(r,i)})).filter(({r})=>(!cat||r.category===cat)&&(!q||`${r.company} ${r.brand} ${r.product} ${r.country} ${r.hall} ${r.booth}`.toLowerCase().includes(q)));host.innerHTML=rows.map(({r,i,key})=>{const pref=exhibitorPrefs[key]||{},important=Boolean(pref.important)||r.status==='مهم للزيارة';return `<article class="exhibitor-card"><div class="exhibitor-top"><div><h4>${esc(r.company||r.brand||'عارض')}</h4>${r.brand&&r.brand!==r.company?`<div class="exhibitor-brand">${esc(r.brand)}</div>`:''}</div><button class="favorite-toggle ${important?'active':''}" data-exhibitor-favorite="${esc(key)}" title="مهم للزيارة" type="button">★</button></div><div class="exhibitor-tags"><span class="exhibitor-tag">${esc(r.category||'غير مصنف')}</span>${r.country?`<span class="exhibitor-tag">${esc(r.country)}</span>`:''}${r.hall||r.booth?`<span class="exhibitor-tag">${esc([r.hall,r.booth].filter(Boolean).join(' • '))}</span>`:''}</div><div class="exhibitor-details">${esc(r.product||'')}${r.owner?`<br><strong>مسؤول المقابلة:</strong> ${esc(r.owner)}`:''}${r.appointment?`<br><strong>الموعد:</strong> ${esc(r.appointment)}`:''}${r.note?`<br><strong>ملاحظة الإدارة:</strong> ${esc(r.note)}`:''}</div><textarea class="exhibitor-note" data-exhibitor-note="${esc(key)}" placeholder="ملاحظتك الشخصية عن هذا العارض">${esc(pref.note||'')}</textarea><div class="exhibitor-actions">${r.site?`<a href="${esc(r.site)}" target="_blank" rel="noopener">الموقع</a>`:''}<button data-exhibitor-calendar="${i}" type="button">إضافة لتقويمي</button>${isAdmin?`<button class="primary" data-exhibitor-program="${i}" type="button">إضافة للب
nent(textValue)}&langpair=${encodeURIComponent(c.source+'|'+c.target)}&mt=1`;
    const res=await fetch(url,{cache:'no-store'});
    if(!res.ok)throw new Error('translation');
    const data=await res.json();
    const translated=decodeTranslationText(data?.responseData?.translatedText||'').trim();
    if(!translated||Number(data?.responseStatus||200)>=400)throw new Error('translation');
    output.value=translated;
    status.textContent='تمت الترجمة.';
  }catch(err){
    console.error(err);
    status.classList.add('error');
    status.textContent='تعذر الاتصال بخدمة الترجمة الآن. حاول مرة أخرى.';
